## HARD CONSTRAINTS + BANKED LESSONS
- **Version SOURCE = the config-singleton unit** (scenario/index/c/o/n/f/i/config-singleton-0000-000000000001.scenario.json model.version); build.mjs write-backs package.json FROM it — NEVER bump package.json directly. The no-flush guard blocks the server re-flushing it.
- **Server change → RESTART + BOOT-CHECK**: `otmux send remoteShells:0.2 "d"` then `"npm start"` + Enter; then `curl /api/config` responds (module-top errors slip past tsx/esbuild transpile). Verify config git-CLEAN after.
- **BASH DENIALS here**: commits with unicode(→/«»)/heredoc/`$()` → PERMISSION-DENIED → Write the commit msg to a scratch .txt + `git commit -F <file>`. Avoid `$()` substitution + compound-with-curl-to-prod. Pure-core verify = esbuild-bundle→`/opt/node22/bin/node` (NOT tsx: node16 chokes on .mts; a `typescript` ESM import won't resolve from scratchpad — ESM ignores NODE_PATH → test logic-only or defer to tester).
- **MARKER discipline (#126)**: NEVER self-mint (req is sole minter); [impl:uuid] must be ADJACENT-ABOVE the name-matching decl (intervening decls STEAL the strict-AST bind — task-275 bug); pre-place VERIFY uuid=ior:class:Implementation + name-token; if a minted unit expects a NAMED decl I built inline, EXTRACT the function to match (don't force a mismatched marker).
- **HONEST-DEFER**: gated-HTTP owner flows + DOM gestures = tester's @390 job; verify what I can (transpile/boot/pure-logic), NEVER claim untested (the (A) enrich bug + R36.3 crash both slipped past 'transpile-clean' — exercise the real path or defer honestly).

- **stash@{0}** = superseded pre-wall revert-WIP (deletes usedIn side-index + universal-actions) — KEEP (recoverable), do NOT pop unless PO confirms.
- Context at rewind = 79% (autocompact OFF). PO/SM manage rewind timing; flag PO if ≥~80% + a fix-block scopes → rewind-FIRST.

**★★★ DOCTRINE (PO 2026-07-30, DURABLE — bank hard): a VERSION-BUMP ALWAYS needs a server RESTART, even client-only. Under R31.7 the /api/config version is BUILD-STAMPED into the server process → after any Config-unit bump + build, /api/config stays STALE (skewed vs SW/manifest = R31.7 INV violation + R31.12 banner-skew) until the server is bounced. So EVERY build I ship with a version bump → FLAG architect restart (remoteShells:0.2), not just server-code changes. My old 'client-only fix = auto-serve, no restart' (R30.x) is SUPERSEDED for anything that bumps the version. [[version-bump-mandatory-on-client-fix]] now implies restart too.**

**★ (SUPERSEDED — see BADGE APPROACH SUPERSEDED above) SESSION-BADGE v0.7.98, commit 57f94ff48**: v0.7.97 was INCOMPLETE (fixed storage, not lookup). Tester r316 precise root: computeBadges keyed by ref.split(':')[1], but a session ref is 'otmuxsession:sess:NAME' where the UUID ITSELF ('sess:NAME', server.ts:909 'sess:'+name) CONTAINS a colon → split(':')[1]='sess' ≠ full uuid → my v0.7.97 nodeChildCount (stored under 'sess:NAME') MISSED → domCount 0. FIX: computeBadges(:516) + prefetchLayer(:531) use refUuid(ref) (after FIRST colon) not split(':')[1] — correct for any colon-in-uuid ref, behavior-identical for normal type:UUID (zero /trace risk). Session badge→2. ★ LESSON: split(':')[1] assumes a colon-free uuid; a uuid that embeds a colon (composite key like 'sess:NAME') breaks it — use refUuid (slice after FIRST colon) for ref→uuid everywhere. [prior v0.7.97 f5c35f94c stored the count under the full uuid — the storage half; this is the lookup half.]

**R30.30 absolute blank-anchor re-sync DONE + LIVE + RENDER-VERIFIED (commit 769e3cf1d, v0.7.42, edit-GTJJPWDH.js) [2026-07-17]**: R30.29 counters PREVENT accumulation but don't SNAP residual to 0 (no re-anchor) → tester found a clean 32px (2-row) misalign at L1823 riding to EOF (741 anchors, non-cumulative). FIX (impl-edit alignPaneRows 17c71adf; computeMergedCenter a0b30550 unchanged, produces centerSeq the pass walks — both markers STAY, no new units): rewrote alignPaneRows into a SINGLE forward pass over centerSeq ({ok}=stable anchor, {cid}=changed) tracking REAL lines rL/rC/rR (→ viewZone afterLineNumber) + VISUAL rows vL/vC/vR. Changed → pad each pane to maxH. Stable → RE-ANCHOR FIRST: pad laggards up to max(vL,vC,vR) before the stable lines → any single-region mis-pad snaps to 0 at the next anchor (self-healing, bounded to one block, deterministic — no getTopForLineNumber). Secondary: pinned lineHeight:19+wordWrap:off on all 3 editors. Content untouched → byte-identical. VERIFIED (playwright, TRUE centerSeq correspondence): 3430 stable lines ALL @0px, max dev 0, full file incl L1823. Committed ATOMICALLY (R30.28). → tester DET-3x + Tron. ★★ VERIFICATION LESSON (critical): assertion-grade alignment MUST use TRUE correspondence (centerSeq stable {ok} segments = same logical line across panes), NOT content-uniqueness — a common line (method signature) recurs at non-corresponding positions and reads as a false 32000px 'deviation'. My first dense check reported 739 fails (all false anchors); the centerSeq-correspondence check reported the truth (0px). Match the oracle to the structure. ★ DESIGN LESSON: "prevents accumulation" (R30.29 counters) ≠ "snaps to 0" (R30.30 re-anchor) — a per-region trust-the-count model freezes any single mis-count; an ABSOLUTE re-anchor at known-good anchors is self-healing + robust to imperfect per-region accounting.
**R30.29 modification-region resync DONE + LIVE + RENDER-VERIFIED (commit 6422be938, v0.7.41, edit-GLDGMB7L.js) [2026-07-17]**: completes R30.27 (LEFT drifted 368px on modifications). Architect r3029: a one-sided MODIFICATION (buffer 'a'/'b', oLength>0) means the NON-changed pane still shows the M=oLength base lines it retains; R30.27 modeled that side empty + never advanced its counter → cumulative drift. FIX (impl-edit computeMergedCenter/computeOneSidedHunks, marker a0b30550 STAYS, no new units): (1) vendor diff3.ts StableRegion exposes oStart/oLength (populated at the single-hunk a/b path from hunk.oStart/oLength + the 'o' advanceTo path from currOffset — 2 fields, no logic). (2) computeOneSidedHunks(region,cid,la,lb,baseLines): non-changed side = baseLines.slice(oStart,oStart+oLength) ([] for insertion oLength=0 → one-sided preserved; M base lines for modification → both panes align maxH=max(N,M), both highlight). (3) advance BOTH counters: changed += bufferLength(N), non-changed += oLength(M) → la/lb track TRUE consumed lines → resync EVERY region (the "empty-line-anchored resync" emerges by construction, NOT a separate loop). VERIFIED LIVE (playwright getTopForLineNumber L/C/R, otmux, 12 anchors incl modification regions): max dev 0px (was 368), ALL ALIGNED. Byte-identical: centerLen==devLen==133728 (CENTER picks changed side; base slice → non-picked side only). Both-band: local 49 (modifications) + remote 60 (all) → insertions one-sided, modifications both. count '60 changes,0 conflicts'. Committed ATOMICALLY with build (R30.28 [[commit-build-atomically-with-deploy]]). → tester hard gate d5f8a2028 DET-3x + Tron. ★ LESSON: the architect framed it as an "anchor-resync" algorithm but the actual impl is just base-slice + advance-both-counters — track each pane's TRUE consumed line count and alignment/resync EMERGES (alignPaneRows pads maxH); don't hand-write a resync loop when correct counters make it automatic.
**R30.27 line-align PARTIAL + design-gap ESCALATED (commit 1e2b2fb6e, v0.7.40, edit-47SU4MGA.js) [2026-07-17]**: implemented architect r3027 fix faithfully (impl-edit computeMergedCenter/computeOneSidedHunks, marker a0b30550 STAYS): thread la/lb per-pane line counters; computeOneSidedHunks(region,cid,la,lb) sets aStart=la/bStart=lb (dropped the R30.23 `0` fallbacks); ok-run+false-conflict advance both, conflict path resyncs la=aStart+aLen/lb=bStart+bLen. VERIFY (playwright getTopForLineNumber L/C/R, otmux deep-link, 12 stable anchors): CENTER==REMOTE now align ±0 + pure insertions align; PARTIAL — LEFT drifts cumulatively (max 368px). ROOT (region-probe): otmux=60 repo-only regions, 50 MODIFICATIONS (oLength>0, 121 base lines) vs 11 insertions. Architect model (la-UNCHANGED + a=[] for buffer 'b') is right for INSERTIONS but for a MODIFICATION the LEFT pane retains the base lines the model drops → drift. NEEDS opposite-side base slice: computeOneSidedHunks non-changed side = base[oStart..+oLength] + advance base-consuming counter; StableRegion lacks oStart/oLength → minimal vendor extension (single-hunk path already has hunk.oStart/oLength). NOT freelancing the vendor/model change (scenario-first) → escalated to architect(0.3) for derive-confirm before building. NOT R30.27-done. ★ PROCESS (memory [[commit-build-atomically-with-deploy]]): SM flagged served-uncommitted PHANTOM 2× (v0.7.38, v0.7.40) — build.mjs IS the deploy; now commit version+dist+sw+source IMMEDIATELY after build, before verify/report; served==committed==HEAD always. ★ LESSON: a faithfully-implemented derived design can still miss its AC on real data — MEASURE against the AC (getTopForLineNumber) on a REPRESENTATIVE input (otmux = mostly modifications, not the insertion-heavy toy case), and when the gap is architectural, escalate with the pinpoint measurement (region-probe: 50 mods/121 lines) rather than freelance.
**R30.25.2 WIN-B residual DONE + LIVE + RENDER-VERIFIED (commit 466dd417d, v0.7.39, edit-7KTKKWFM.js) [2026-07-17]**: PO found the R30.25.1 guard was checked BEFORE loadSide('right'), so a pick landing INSIDE the deep-link's OWN right-load still clobbered (dev resolves last → content=dev under ref=pick = RIGHT-corrupt ref/content split). FIX (impl-edit, loadSide c4da837c STAYS): per-right-load generation counter _rightLoadSeq — each loadSide('right') bumps + captures at entry; after the fetch await, if seq !== this._rightLoadSeq (a newer right-load started) → DISCARD (return before setValue/content/recompute). Last right-load wins, ref+content always agree. LEFT NOT seq-checked → a right-pick can't abort a left-load. VERIFIED LIVE: pick RIGHT=prod during the deep-link's delayed dev-load → rightRef=prod, content=62748 (prod not dev 133728), editor==state (no split), left non-empty. No regression (normal→dev; pick-during-left-load→user wins). All 3 deep-link right-pick windows closed. BUG-1 RIGHT-corrupt now FULLY closed; BUG-2 LEFT-empty still not-reproducible → awaiting Tron. ★ LESSON: a guard checked BEFORE an awaited call only covers races that land before the call starts — races landing DURING the call need the guard INSIDE the awaited op (last-writer-wins → generation counter checked post-await). Two windows (before-call + during-call) need two guards.
**R30.25.1 DONE + LIVE + RENDER-VERIFIED (commit b19b492c1, v0.7.38, edit-QTSMTUQQ.js) [2026-07-17]**: tester (gating R30.25) found a SIBLING race in R30.24 openFromParams — its `await loadSide('right', dev)` had NO token/_rightUserPicked guard, so a user RIGHT-pick DURING the deep-link's left-load got clobbered when the deep-link's in-flight right-load resolved last (RIGHT corrupts). FIX (impl-edit, marker dc236c19 STAYS): openFromParams resets _rightUserPicked + takes a generation token (++_promoteToken) BEFORE its awaits; after the left-load, `if (token !== this._promoteToken || this._rightUserPicked) return` → skips the deep-link right-load, honoring the user pick (same pattern as populateLeftHistory). VERIFIED LIVE (playwright): (1) normal deep-link → rightRef=dev, left loaded (round-trip); (2) race → rightRef=main (user won, dev aborted), left non-empty; (3) PO-caution LEFT-EMPTY re-verified @0.7.38 — R30.25 Case B byte-identical 2348→2348, Case A non-empty (no regression). oosh 'main' lacks otmux → empty right title in case2 (test-ref artifact; rightRef=main still proves the fix). ★ LESSON: when you add a guard-pattern (token/_rightUserPicked) to ONE async writer of shared state, AUDIT every OTHER async writer of the same state (populateLeftHistory AND openFromParams both write this.right across awaits) — the tester caught the un-guarded sibling. Also: heed a reviewer's CAUTION — the tester's repro proved 'left byte-identical' but NOT Tron's 'left EMPTY'; I re-verified the actual symptom separately.

PO precise: my 'left byte-identical' GREEN ≠ reproducing the BLANK-left RED. ★ I MEASURED Tron's EXACT flow on v0.7.38 (playwright prod, open /edit/otmux?repo=oosh&left=516ebb3&right=dev → settle → change RIGHT dev→prod): LEFT stayed 119373 byte-identical NON-empty, RIGHT→prod, recompute OK, NO errors — LEFT NEVER BLANKED. (2) when a fix can't reproduce the reported RED, suspect a stale-cache/environmental delta + defer to the reporter's re-test rather than declaring done. **R30.25 DONE + LIVE + RENDER-VERIFIED (commit ec5d62dee, v0.7.37, edit-P6E3RCW4.js) [2026-07-17]**: fix left-empties — a RIGHT (Repository) ref/branch pick was blanking the LEFT (Local) editor. Tron invariant: a RIGHT pick touches ONLY right+center, never left. ROOT (architect): asymmetric race — R30.17 populateLeftHistory auto-promote fired fire-and-forget, no _rightUserPicked guard, read live this.right.content mid-flight; a RIGHT pick landing during the promote re-derived from the mutated this.right + reloaded LEFT, racing the pick's computeMergedCenter → LEFT blank. IMPL-EDITS (no new units, markers STAY; req a604a1b5 mint 529795387, architect derive 72cbf9f49): setSideRef[pickRef f0b7ef57] a RIGHT pick sets symmetric _rightUserPicked + bumps _promoteToken (WINS + invalidates in-flight promote); loadSide[c4da837c] AWAITs populateLeftHistory (serialize) + resets _rightUserPicked on a fresh LEFT working-load (BEFORE the content-load awaits, so a later user RIGHT pick re-sets it and wins); populateLeftHistory[751934c1] early-guard (if _rightUserPicked return), generation token + this.left.content SNAPSHOT before awaits, aborts LEFT-reload tail on token/flag mismatch after each await, defaultIdx from snapshot not live this.right. RENDER PROOF (playwright prod, package.json): CASE B (pick RIGHT after promote) LEFT byte-identical 2348→2348 RIGHT→ref; CASE A (pick RIGHT during promote's fetch awaits, window widened via page.route delay) LEFT preserved byte-for-byte + RIGHT pick WON (rightRef=main), never blanked. ★★ TWO LESSONS: (1) placement of a reset matters w.r.t. the await schedule — v0.7.36 reset at populateLeftHistory TOP kept LEFT alive but let the promote OVERWRITE the RIGHT pick (loadSide awaits computeMergedCenter before the promote, so a same-tick pick set the flag first, then the top-reset wiped it); moving the reset to loadSide's fresh-load top + an early-guard made RIGHT win. Caught by my OWN repro asserting rightRef, not just 'left non-empty'. (2) to test an async RACE deterministically, WIDEN the window with page.route latency on the in-flight fetches, and only fire the racing action once its precondition holds (right.path set by the promote's synchronous top — setSideRef('right') bails without a path). **R30.23 DONE + LIVE + SERVED-VERIFIED (commit 67c8793d2, v0.7.33, edit-BMERY5NZ.js) [2026-07-16]**: diff-completeness — one-sided auto-merged changes now VISIBLE (Tron IMG_4522: a 'merged, 0 conflicts' file rendered ZERO change blocks). IMPL-EDIT to RbDiffEditor.computeMergedCenter (marker a0b30550 STAYS; req mint ddc01b2e0; NO new minted units — PO reconciled req↔design: computeOneSidedHunks = PRIVATE helper, NOT a minted Method, since one-sided detection is the same concern computeMergedCenter already owns). MECHANISM: the 3-way branch iterates the RICHER diff3MergeRegions (I EXPORTED it + Region/StableRegion types from vendor/diff3.ts — export-ONLY, zero logic change) instead of the collapsed diff3Merge — because diff3Merge folds every stable region into flat {ok:[…]}, DISCARDING the per-region `buffer` origin tag. Each StableRegion.buffer: 'o'=truly stable(==base)→ok-run · 'a'=local-only change(diff3 auto-applied)→Conflict{kind:'change',pick:'a',a=bufferContent,b=[],aStart=bufferStart} · 'b'=repo-only→{pick:'b',a=[],b=bufferContent,bStart}. The buffer tag IS the base comparison, precomputed inside diff3 via diffIndices(o,a)/(o,b). false-conflict (both sides identical change) stays an ok-run (not one-sided, no double-count); true divergence stays kind:'conflict' (unchanged, aStart/bStart=region.aStart/bStart). ORIGIN-EXACT + downstream FREE: renderSideChangeBlocks/renderConnectorRibbons ALREADY origin-gate on a.length>0(Local)/b.length>0(Repo), renderCenterChangeBlocks uses c.span (set by rebuildCenter), jumpToChange counts — so setting a=content,b=[] (local) vs a=[],b=content (repo) renders each side origin-exact with NO new rendering code. RESULT BYTE-IDENTICAL (visibility-only): rebuildCenter picks c.pick==='b'?c.b:c.a = exactly the content diff3 folded into center; self-test (local b→B2 + repo d→D2 vs base a,b,c,d,e) → regions o,a,o,b,o, center==[a,B2,c,D2,e] byte-identical to auto-merge, both one-sided surfaced local@1+repo@3. Typecheck clean, build GREEN, prod auto-served 0.7.33 (edit bundle rotated, computeOneSidedHunks present live). → tester gate + Tron IMG_4522. **R30.23.1 label fix (commit 23a927aff, v0.7.34, edit-W5ST5LB6.js)**: tester flagged that kind:'change' one-sided hunks live in conflicts[] → BOTH count sites over-reported ('2 changes, 2 conflicts' + status '2 conflicts to resolve' when 0 TRUE conflicts). FIX mode-aware 2×1-line: de-count nc2 = filter((twoWay||kind==='conflict') && !dismissed) + status nc = filter(kind==='conflict') → '2 changes, 0 conflicts' + 'clean auto-merge'. 2-way kept counting all take-overs (there every hunk is kind:'change'). LESSON: when you ADD a new kind into a shared collection (conflicts[]), audit EVERY consumer that counts/labels off that collection — a counter that was correct pre-change silently mis-reports. ★★ LESSON (durable): when a library COLLAPSES structure you need (diff3Merge flattens buffer-tagged regions into {ok}), reach one level DOWN to the richer producer (diff3MergeRegions) and EXPORT it rather than re-deriving the classification by hand — the origin was already computed, just discarded at the collapse. And: a visibility-only feature must prove RESULT-INVARIANCE (byte-identical merge) as its own test, separate from the visibility assertion. [[reproduce-when-code-reads-single]]

★★★ TEAM RULE BANKED (req/PO, R30.6.7 — SYSTEMATIC bug caught): a code [impl:uuid:<U>] marker's <U> is the IMPLEMENTATION unit uuid, NEVER the Method uuid. The Method's implementations[] holds the Impl uuid on disk; the CODE marker points at the Impl. Using the Method uuid mis-attaches credit to the Method + leaves the Impl unlinked. MY CHECK FAILED to catch it (I verified set.has(<the uuid I placed>) which trivially passes) — VERIFY the IMPL unit credits, and confirm each uuid's ior:class is Implementation (not Method) BEFORE placing. The PO handed me Method uuids labeled as Impls (build-go), I placed 3; req's marker-gate caught it. Also: a new class goes in its OWN file matching the minted Impl sourceFile (not the server.ts monolith) — #126 sourceFile is authoritative over a verbal "put it in server.ts".

RIGHT side defaults to the file git-history. GitApi.fileHistory [impl e9cfaab3] (git log --follow -- path, safeRelPath NOT guardRef — it is a path; route /api/git/file-history?repo=&path=) + RbDiffEditor.populateRightHistory [impl 58c11039] (on LOCAL load → .de-history select newest-first → auto-load newest into RIGHT; older pick → loadSide; no-history → ⎇ fallback). 2/2 credit. ★ BANKED RULE CAUGHT AGAIN (2nd time): PO build-go gave the METHOD uuids (0e3f8c07/3dd2173b); I verified ior:class + placed the Impls (e9cfaab3/58c11039). Flagged PO to source Method.implementations[0] up front. Server change → restart.

Architect post-build confirm PASS (8/8, 3 superseded removed, 0 orphan Impls). → tester DET-3x + req per-method + Tron visual. ★ DIFF-EDITOR ARC COMPLETE my side: R30.6→6.6→6.7→7→9 all shipped+live+verified. IntelliJ 3-way merge, re-arch of rb-diff-editor (textarea/LCS→3-Monaco+diff3). 8/8 markers credit (all verified ior:class:Implementation FIRST — banked rule): monacoLoader e09a8327·mountThreePane c4c84142·computeMergedCenter a0b30550·renderMergeGutter e24dc98a·acceptChange 843d79d4·applyAllNonConflicting 91c452ae·syncScroll3 e3431e87·GitApi.mergeBase b17ad1df. LESSON: for a lib dep, VENDOR the faithful upstream source (obtain via npm install → copy → uninstall) rather than hand-roll (my hand-rolled diff3 had the classic adjacent-region bug) OR keep the npm dep (prod-build-deps risk); vendoring = proven algo + self-contained bundle. ## Chain crediting rule (buildStrictImplSet, skill-classes.ts:135-167) — MEASURED 2026-06-29
An `[impl:uuid:U label]` credits U ONLY if: (1) idx.has(U) [unit exists], AND (2) the marker
HEADS or sits INSIDE a NAMED member (function/method/field-arrow) whose name name-matches the
label's method-token (equality OR substring either direction; `labelMethod = last '.'-segment of
first non-R/FLAG token`). Anon-arrow host, data-const/prop host, or file-head (→ first decl) = NO
credit. The Method/Impl UNIT name is IRRELEVANT to crediting — only the SOURCE marker's label +
host member. refCount>1 (impl shared by >1 Method) = NEVER credited. A Method with N impls needs
ALL N to credit+test → de-dup to ONE. Tools: Chain.wireImplNode(methodUuid) mints fresh impl
named after Method + wires; Chain.renameUuid(old) atomic 3-sweep (unit+refs+source/test/md markers).
Run via node18 (/root/.vscode-server/bin/*/node) + node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/objectVerb.ts Chain <verb> (npx tsx fails on node16).

DRY: RbPanZoom reusable.

## Contact-unit pattern (R21.3/4/5/6 — REUSE for R21.7/8)
- alt-UUID index: `alt/<kind>/<normalizedKey>.scenario.json` symlink → declared on the
  PROFILE unit's unitLinks[] so ensureSymlinkDisk targets the profile file. Lookup =
  fs.readFileSync(linkPath) → JSON.parse → model.uuid. (Declare link on the unit you want
  the key to RESOLVE TO.)
- First-class unit + relationship: `<Index>.mintAndLink(profileUuid, raw, uuid)` mints
  ior:class:<Kind> { normalizedField, ownerIor:profile }, pushes ior:instance:<uuid> into
  Profile.model.<kind>s[] (multiple, idempotent dedup by normalized value), then registerSymlink.
- Caller passes the v4 uuid (crypto.randomUUID() server-side) — keeps the shared scenario
  module crypto-free (client-bundle safe).
- Server: indexProfilePhone/indexProfileEmail ensure a Profile unit exists then mintAndLink;
  called from committed UPDATE_PROFILE; self-healing, wrapped, never blocks save.
- R21.4 device-link: resolveKeyToProfile(phone,email) → PhoneIndex/EmailIndex.resolveToProfile.
  IDENTIFY sends KNOWN_KEY_CHALLENGE before mint; DEVICE_ENROLL_REQUEST{profileUuid} validates
  secretCode vs that profile → enroll new device + TOKEN_REDIRECT.

- ★ SEAM3 RULED (PO) = OPTION A compute-on-read CONTENT-HASH revision (DERIVE currency, don't mirror). Rationale: a controller-bumped counter only tracks CONTROLLER changes → misses CLI mint/migrate/generate + federation + generator + human file edits → false-currency = the silent-drift C4 kills. A content-hash can't miss a writer (derived from content). BUILD-TIME CONSTRAINTS (4): (1) REUSE federation's EXISTING contentHash — NO 2nd hasher (two defs of 'same content' = two-sources disease). (2) Hash the CANONICAL serialization (stable key order + whitespace) so it can't FLAP → false staleness. (3) FAIL-CLOSED: no hash / unknown / unreachable compare = STALE never fresh (NOT-RUN==RED shape). (4) NO sequence numbers — hash-mismatch IS the staleness signal; reconcile-on-focus/reconnect + hash-compare-on-resume covers missed events (drop the sequence-gap hardening). Simplifies seam1-hardening (A): removes schema change + migration + chokepoint write. Architect folds+confirms the shape. ★ EXPERT: NO code until req mints C4 StepEvidence (evidenceForStep, the shared predicate) — HOLD. blocked-on-mint.
